    SELECT TOP(@__p_1) [h].[Id], [h].[Abogado], [h].[Cliente], [h].[Fecha], [h].[IdDocumento], [h].[TipoDocumento], [h].[Titulo]
    FROM [HistorialDocumentos] AS [h]
    WHERE [h].[Abogado] = @__cedulaAbogado_0
    ORDER BY [h].[Fecha] DESC
) AS [t]
LEFT JOIN [T_GeAbogados] AS [t0] ON [t].[Abogado] = [t0].[Cedula]
LEFT JOIN [T_GePersonas] AS [t1] ON [t0].[Cedula] = [t1].[Cedula]
LEFT JOIN [T_CrDistritos] AS [t2] ON [t1].[Direccion1] = [t2].[idDistrito]
LEFT JOIN [T_GePersonas] AS [t3] ON [t].[Cliente] = [t3].[Cedula]
LEFT JOIN [T_CrDistritos] AS [t4] ON [t3].[Direccion1] = [t4].[idDistrito]
ORDER BY [t].[Fecha] DESC
